# Issue #646 tracked site manifest

Evidence tree: `05fef1cfb8d560a234c9aeab3ede09600517daa3` (7 September 2026).

This manifest covers every AST candidate occurrence in non-test `.ts`/`.tsx` files under `src`, `server/src`, and `shared/src`. Tests, specs, `__tests__`, and fixture directories are excluded from syntax categories; source directives are separately inventoried across every tracked typed file because negative type tests are part of that category. A site key is the stable evidence-tree `file:line:column/symbol`; line and occurrence totals are deliberately separate.

## Reproduction

The temporary generator was run as `node /private/tmp/generate-646-manifest.cjs` from the evidence
worktree (SHA-256 `86b7679ae89689dffff22f922825145b53f38dfdfa8b5e0ee1aa92bcf11f5d7c`). It
parses TypeScript/TSX with TypeScript 6.0.3; it does not grep for `as`, boolean operators, or
constructor substrings. Directives and debt markers are scanned only in comment trivia/text. The
async inventory uses the typed rule itself:

```sh
pnpm exec eslint 'src/**/*.{ts,tsx}' 'server/src/**/*.{ts,tsx}' 'shared/src/**/*.{ts,tsx}' \
  --ignore-pattern '**/*.test.*' --ignore-pattern '**/*.spec.*' \
  --ignore-pattern '**/__tests__/**' --ignore-pattern '**/fixtures/**' \
  --rule '@typescript-eslint/require-await:error' --format json
```

The generated Markdown is the only tracked artifact; the temporary probe is not a repository tool.

| Category                              | Occurrences | Lines | Terminal disposition                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------- | ----------: | ----: | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Record<string, unknown>               |         220 |   196 | Deferred tracked debt owned by C6-RU; syntax alone cannot establish boundary safety.         |
| native throw new Error                |         258 |   258 | Accepted individually by native-error role; syntax alone does not prove taxonomy misuse.     |
| console member reference              |         123 |   123 | Accepted individually as visible diagnostics/breadcrumbs; no blanket logging rule.           |
| async function without await          |          47 |    47 | Accepted Promise-interface debt; require-await remains unenrolled.                           |
| type assertion                        |         771 |   691 | Deferred tracked debt owned by C6-AS; lint removes only the mechanically unnecessary subset. |
| satisfies expression                  |          32 |    32 | Accepted compile-time conformance checks.                                                    |
| boolean-literal comparison            |         110 |   107 | Accepted after the enrolled typed rule; the actionable comparison was removed by #645.       |
| String/Number coercion                |         161 |   141 | Accepted individually; no syntax-only coercion rule.                                         |
| TODO/FIXME/HACK marker                |           0 |     0 | Fixed/absent when zero; otherwise actionable.                                                |
| eslint-disable directive              |           6 |     6 | Accepted individually with local rationale.                                                  |
| @ts-ignore/@ts-expect-error directive |           4 |     4 | Accepted only when checked @ts-expect-error; @ts-ignore is actionable.                       |
